# Plain words on sold screens and in LINE — owner step 6

Status: REVIEW DRAFT — controller audit limits closed, current free/paid audiences traced, implementation and reviewed-help approval pending · Re-anchored to develop e7c422802 on 2026-11-04 · controller accepted round 3 on 2026-11-04 (B18 daily retirement and B23 owner exception recorded outside wording); owner answers recorded 2026-11-04
Owner: thawatchai (product wording); documents lane (code inventory and proposed wording)
Related: `docs/plans/plain-words-sold-screens-plan.md`; `docs/guidelines/thai-ux-standard.md`; `docs/plans/sold-ux-owner-decisions-2026-10-13-plan.md`; `docs/agent-memory/expense-leadership-specs-2026-10-23.md`; `docs/specs/expense-payment-preparation-voucher-spec-th.md`
Baseline: develop `e7c4228026eec3c696fa76986420d2c97cb26676` in worktree `expense-specs-20261023`, re-opened 2026-11-04. Original audit `0f938a36154c5bfbd8018b7bdafd8b18e3fd8df2` is retained as history.

## 1. Goal and boundaries

Owner, 2026-10-26: “ระบบมั่งมีพลัส ใช้ง่ายเป็นผู้นำด้านเราจ่าย คนไม่รู้บัญชี ภาษีก็ใช้ได้ ไม่จำเป็นต้องมีศัพท์ทางบัญชีสื่อสารกับยูเซอร์ แต่มีศัพท์ทางบัญชีภาษีในรีพอร์ทที่ส่งสำนักงานบัญชีและสรรพากรได้”. Recorded first in `docs/agent-memory/expense-leadership-specs-2026-10-23.md:49`; step 6 is at `:59` (“replace accounting jargon on sold screens and in LINE”).

The reader is a shop employee or owner who has never studied accounting. F sends a supported document, checks its source draft and keeps edits. P continues through confirmation and payment. The paid journey stays: send/enter an expense → check its facts and evidence → confirm → record an actual payment → give the accountant the existing files. This work changes presentation words. It does not change a tax answer, tax calculation, evidence requirement, permission, notification recipient, charge, final confirmation, stored value, or export.

R1. Apply to the current sold ceiling including eligible never-paid free source drafts (F) and the paid packs (P). F uses the existing restricted source-draft access and model-free commands. All paid packs use starter; 1,999 adds `wht_pnd53` and `wht_50bis` (`packages/contracts/src/menu-entitlements.ts:433` “dashboard_notifications”, `:496` “TIER_FEATURE_PRESETS”, `:500` “wht_pnd53”). A role alone is not an entitlement. Keep every existing permission and company entry setting. Add no permission, environment switch, dependency, schema change or migration.

R2. Use the one word table in §3 and the exact context-specific sentences in §4. Do not perform a global string replacement. A short label, explanation, error, accessible name, guide quote and LINE card must name the same thing. One message is one short sentence; an error/refusal has an available way forward on that screen/card. Retain the LINE voice and the web's neutral voice.

The permission-only settings filter is not proof that every card is sold: its inputs are already effective session grants (`settings-menu.logic.ts:146` “canSeeRoute”; `WorkspaceLayout.tsx:294` “session.permissions”). Tax capture inside an expense is in scope even though the full tax workspace is unsold (`expense-capture-policy.ts:59` “showsTaxCapture”). Historical locks can also refuse a sold mutation; those error messages remain in scope.

## 2. Inventory method and how to read the occurrence ledger

Search all web source, contracts, the API LINE module and Gateway LINE module, then trace each owning component/helper and its caller. Search terms:

```text
ภาษีซื้อ|สิทธิภาษีซื้อ|สิทธิ์ภาษีซื้อ|เครดิตภาษี|ลงบัญชี|บันทึกบัญชี|ผังบัญชี|
ใบสำคัญจ่าย|ฐานภาษี|ฐาน VAT|ฐานหัก|งวดภาษี|งวดบัญชี|ภ.ง.ด.|ภงด|ภ.พ.|
ใบกำกับภาษี|หัก ณ ที่จ่าย|หักภาษี ณ ที่จ่าย|50 ทวิ|VAT|ภาษีมูลค่าเพิ่ม|
ตั้งหนี้|เจ้าหนี้|ลูกหนี้|เดบิต|เครดิต|ปิดงวด|นำส่ง|ยื่นแบบ|ผู้มีหน้าที่|
เงินได้|ประเภทเงินได้|ภาษีขาย|ภาษีซื้อต้องห้าม|ค่ารับรอง|ภาษี|Tax Code|
Tax Profile|JV|ผ่านรายการ|กลับรายการ|สมุดรายวัน|กระทบยอด|ต้นทุน|
ใบสำคัญรับเงิน|เลขประจำตัวผู้เสียภาษี|รหัสสาขาภาษี|เงินเพิ่ม|เบี้ยปรับ
```

Use literal matching for `ภ.พ.` and `ภ.ง.ด.`; optional punctuation searches also require a form number, avoiding the false match “ยุทธภพ”. Search English identifiers separately to find imported labels and dynamic values; identifiers, regex matchers, model/OCR/router prompts, tests, fixtures, comments and customer-entered names are not fixed customer copy. No network research was allowed or used. Paper-name justifications below are checked against local paper/export implementations, not new legal advice.

The ledger in §10 records source occurrences, including retained terms and rejected candidates. Several occurrences can compose one message, and one literal can serve several screens. `K` means the current text is the proposed text; `Cnn` uses §4's exact new sentence; `Wnn` uses the one word table without changing a fact; `X` records an exclusion and its reason. S/O/N in the classified ledger supersede the original candidate markers. A shared label's consumers inherit its ledger row; screen and export consumers are distinguished in §5. Dynamic values such as custom tax-code/category names are inventoried at their rendering seam, not guessed from production data.

## 3. One word table (wording source of truth)

R3. Replace tax-right jargon without promising a refund or inferring eligibility from a VAT amount. Keep `CLAIMABLE/NON_CLAIMABLE/NEEDS_REVIEW`, conditions and review actions unchanged.

R4. Explain monetary fields in the terms of the paper while preserving every amount, rate, unit, base and zero treatment. “ยอดก่อน VAT” is not automatically the withholding base and is not interchangeable with the amount paid.

| Word id | Term / classification | Screen and LINE wording | Exact output term / reason |
|---|---|---|---|
| W01 | สิทธิภาษีซื้อ / สิทธิ์ภาษีซื้อ — **replace** | ใช้ VAT ตอนยื่นภาษี | สิทธิภาษีซื้อ; output headers at `packages/contracts/src/expenses.ts:1863` |
| W02 | ภาษีซื้อ / เครดิตภาษีซื้อ / เครดิต VAT — **replace** | VAT ที่นำไปหักตอนยื่นภาษี; verb: นำ VAT ไปหักตอนยื่นภาษี | ภาษีซื้อ / เครดิตภาษีซื้อ; never say “ได้เงินคืน” |
| W03 | ภาษีซื้อขอคืนได้ / ขอคืนไม่ได้ — **replace** | ใช้ VAT ตอนยื่นภาษีได้ / ใช้ VAT ตอนยื่นภาษีไม่ได้ | Preserve stored Tax Code `displayName`; screen presentation only, see §5 |
| W04 | ภาษีซื้อต้องห้าม — **replace** | VAT ที่กฎไม่ให้ใช้ตอนยื่นภาษี | ภาษีซื้อต้องห้าม; retain the existing example ค่ารับรอง, explained “ค่าเลี้ยงรับรอง” |
| W05 | ลงบัญชี / บันทึกบัญชี (journal meaning) — **replace** | Context-specific C14/C15/C34; use “ส่งให้ผู้ทำบัญชีตรวจต่อ” only as an instruction | ลงบัญชี / บันทึกบัญชี; never claim that sending or posting happened |
| W06 | บันทึกบัญชี (bank-account save) — **replace** | บันทึกบัญชีรับเงินคืน | บัญชีรับเงินคืน; this is not journal posting (`ReimbursementPayout.tsx:384`) |
| W07 | ฐานภาษี / ฐาน VAT — **replace** | ยอดที่ใช้คิด VAT; only use ยอดก่อน VAT where that is the actual field | ฐานภาษี (`expenses.ts:1842`); do not change calculations |
| W08 | ฐานหัก / ฐานคำนวณหัก ณ ที่จ่าย — **replace** | ยอดที่ใช้คิดหัก ณ ที่จ่าย (บาท) | ฐานหัก ณ ที่จ่าย; maintain explicit custom base |
| W09 | งวดภาษี — **replace** | เดือนที่ยื่นภาษี, with the actual month; unknown period: รอบภาษี | งวดภาษี; never invent a form or month |
| W10 | งวดบัญชี / ปิดงวด / ผ่านรายการ / กลับรายการ / JV — **replace** on reachable refusal only | Use C34 with the requested action and existing accountant/support route | Exact accounting terms stay in outputs and unsold ledger screens |
| W11 | ประเภทเงินได้ / หมวดเงินได้ — **replace** | จ่ายค่าอะไร; result: รายการที่จ่าย | ประเภทเงินได้ (`withholding-accountant-export.ts:32`) |
| W12 | เงินได้ / ฐานเงินได้ — **replace** | ยอดที่จ่าย when it means gross paid; ยอดที่ใช้คิดหัก ณ ที่จ่าย when it means the base | เงินได้; verify the specific DTO field before using either phrase |
| W13 | นำส่ง — **replace** in explanatory prose | ส่งภาษีให้กรมสรรพากร | นำส่ง; never say the app sent it |
| W14 | ยื่นแบบ — **keep but explain once** | ยื่นแบบ — ส่งแบบให้กรมสรรพากร | ยื่นแบบ; action and status must distinguish download from actual filing (`WithholdingPage.tsx:34`) |
| W15 | หัก ณ ที่จ่าย — **keep but explain once** | หัก ณ ที่จ่าย; one line: “เงินที่หักจากผู้รับเงินไว้ส่งให้กรมสรรพากร” | ภาษีหัก ณ ที่จ่าย; matches certificate/form columns (`withholding-accountant-export.ts:32`) |

| W17 | ใบกำกับภาษี / (เต็มรูป) / อย่างย่อ — **keep on screen** | Same document names; never merge abbreviated and full | Matches paper supplied by the seller; `packages/contracts/src/expenses.ts:471`; `expenses.ts:79` explicitly separates the abbreviated type |
| W18 | VAT — **keep on screen** | VAT; amount labels “VAT ตามเอกสาร” and “VAT ที่ระบบคำนวณ” stay distinct | Matches the bill; expressly permitted brand/unit word in `thai-ux-standard.md:20` |
| W19 | ภาษีมูลค่าเพิ่ม — **replace** on expense screen | VAT | ภาษีมูลค่าเพิ่ม; paper/export wording remains (`expenses.ts:1843`) |
| W20 | ภ.ง.ด.3 / ภ.ง.ด.53 — **keep on screen** | Same dotted names on the sold page; “จ่ายให้บุคคล” / “จ่ายให้บริษัท” where currently supported | Local RD master-based paper implementation: `pnd3-official-form.logic.ts:113`, `pnd53-official-form.logic.ts:142`; no new tax routing rule |
| W21 | ภ.ง.ด.2 — **keep but explain once** in existing exceptional-payee refusal | “ประเภทนี้ใช้ ภ.ง.ด.2 ให้ผู้ทำบัญชีจัดทำแยก” | Existing exception, not a new sold filing feature (`WithholdingPage.tsx:209`) |
| W22 | ภ.พ.36 / ใบเสร็จกรมศุลกากร (VAT นำเข้า) — **keep on screen** as an expense's paper type | Same paper name | `packages/contracts/src/expenses.ts:470`; this does not open the unsold VAT workspace |

## 4. Per-surface changes and truth in each case

R5. Expense upload/manual/list/categories/evidence use plain words; hints do not promise successful AI reading, eligibility or automatic posting. R6. Voucher names and the approved prepare → approve → pay journey stay; explanations follow the actual status. R7. Claims distinguish bank accounts from accounting; errors explain the existing lock. R8. Home, bell, employee/role and shop/settings surfaces use the same names without changing audience. R9. Keep statutory names on the 1,999 filing/certificate screens, simplifying the surrounding instructions. R10. Fixed LINE replies/cards use the same plain concepts and existing permitted actions. R11. Guides/help follow changed screen controls; approved help text is a separate owner gate.

For retained occurrences (§10 K), new text is exactly the current text. For the following changes, one row is one message/label; placeholders denote existing data, never new inferences. Keep current visibility conditions unless explicitly marked deferred. The C rows are proposals, not descriptions of implemented behaviour.

| Change | Source actually opened / failing input → wrong output today | New text, case and truth condition | Smallest edit / things that can break |
|---|---|---|---|
| C01 | `packages/contracts/src/expenses.ts:490` “สิทธิภาษีซื้อ”; reviewer opens a VAT-bearing receipt → jargon label | ใช้ VAT ตอนยื่นภาษี | Change this title constant only; do not change export headings/status labels |
| C02 | `ExpenseInputVatRight.tsx:77` “เลือกประเภทเอกสาร”; unclassified document, reviewer | เลือกประเภทเอกสารแล้วกด “ถัดไป” เพื่อตรวจ VAT | Existing reviewer branch; Next still saves document type |
| C03 | Same `:77`, reader cannot review | ผู้ตรวจจะเลือกประเภทเอกสารก่อนตรวจ VAT | Existing read-only branch, no unavailable button instruction |

Paths without prefixes in §4 are uniquely named files in the occurrence ledger (§10). C34 is a sentence family parameterized by the existing requested action; e.g. “ยกเลิกใบเบิกไม่ได้ ให้ผู้ทำบัญชีตรวจรายการนี้ต่อ”. It is not permission to suppress the specific safe correction path already available. Keep the month/form and a same-screen permitted action/contact, not several competing explanatory paragraphs.

### Surface × case truth matrix (required fixtures)

| Case | Applicable surfaces | Required displayed fact / way out | Forbidden implication |
|---|---|---|---|
| Loading VAT policy or choices | Manual/review/selector | Existing loading state, no premature no-data sentence | “ร้านไม่ได้จด VAT” while unknown/hidden |
| Not registered on the document date | Manual/detail | C26 or existing fixed registration fact; record expense still available | No expense can be recorded |
| Unknown registration, person may answer / may not answer | Review/profile | Existing settings link / instruction naming authorized person, preserve return anchor | Give a non-manager an unusable setup button |
| Ordinary receipt / abbreviated tax invoice / substitute evidence | Review/detail/LINE | C09–C11 and existing evidence status | Receipt usability implies VAT eligibility |
| Full invoice, needs review / eligible conditions complete | Review/detail | C01 + existing needs-review / conditional status | VAT exists, therefore eligible or refunded |
| Existing decision invalidated / previously posted / historical record | Review/detail | C12/C14 and real blocker; contact or permitted correction | Can edit by reversing a journal or unlocking a filed form |
| Buyer mismatch with header-owned / line-owned tax | Review | C31 / C32 with actual header mark/line-edit action | Header mark changes line tax contribution |
| VAT included / excluded; 0%, exempt, non-VAT, custom or mixed rates; override | Manual/review/LINE explain | C23–C28; separate base, VAT, gross and net | All zero treatments are the same; total-before-VAT always equals WHT base |
| WHT absent / explicit no / amount / percent / custom base | Review/LINE calculator/voucher | Existing options and units; C25/C48 only in corresponding branch | Infer a rate, base or actual payment from a missing value |
| 249–999 / 1,999; issuer / reader / no certificate grant | External/system certificate screens | Outside record/history remains usable; only entitled issuer gets issuance; keep paper name | A lower pack cannot record its external paper; all readers can issue |
| Voucher draft / approved waiting / paid / cancelled | Voucher list/document/dialogs/bell/LINE | W24 before pay, W23 after paid; cancelled retains its own state | All voucher documents prove actual payment or every draft is unpaid in external reality |
| PND3 / PND53 / exceptional PND2; downloaded / filed / additional paper | Withholding/LINE | Existing form names and paper-specific state; W14 explanation | Download = filing; 50 ทวิ = remittance; universal deadline |
| Own payout / somebody else's account / changed after submission | Claims/settings/notice | W06, existing owner notification, existing frozen claim account | “บัญชี” means journal or submitted claim account automatically changes |
| Supported signed LINE action / unsupported fallback / failure | LINE | Actual applied/preview/refused state; fallback C50 only; existing web action | All approvals/payments are web-only, or failed call was charged |


### Follow-up change rows (same requirements; no new features)

| Change | Concrete failing case and opened evidence | Full proposed text / smallest seam | Step · first test · acceptance |

This is a small shared word, not a translation engine. |

Preserve explicit financial correction alternatives. |

Review affected source/body/hash together, after owner approval; never falsify approval or merely refresh hashes to pass. Both legacy v4 and active v6 source references are checked. |

“One word per thing” does not mean a screen label must overwrite an accountant column. The existing UI title, stable code, stored legal label and output header serve different audiences. Shared screen/LINE words go in the smallest existing word object; fixed sentences remain with their existing context-aware formatter. No model prompts, classifiers, matchers or user-entered text are rewritten.

## 6. W00 and whole-chain impact

R13. Each wording round updates its focused tests, guide quotes and journey pins together and proves web/API/LINE/output consistency. Focused specs plus typecheck are the light protocol; no legacy role gates and no deploy in this task.

W00 builds hashes from explicit references, operation references and API intake references (`apps/api/src/modules/line-ai/line-ai-capability-manifest.ts:613` “sourceHashes”). The frozen artifact `docs/research/mangmee-ai-capability-manifest-2026-09-27.json` currently lists exactly these web files:

- `apps/web/src/features/reimbursements/ReimbursementDraftActionPage.tsx` — explicit reference at manifest `:255`.
- `apps/web/src/features/reports/ReportsWorkspacePage.tsx` — explicit reference at `:254`; unsold and unchanged.
- `apps/web/src/features/user-guide/getting-started-guide.content.ts` — operation source reference at `:160`.

It does **not** hash every web file. It also hashes `packages/contracts/src/menu-entitlements.ts` through `MENU_CATALOG_PATH` (`:226`), expense-action service (`:240`), voucher service (`:243`), webhook (`:232`), and further tool source references. After the final source edits, run the existing update mode once, review the actual artifact diff, then rerun frozen parity without update. The test at `line-ai-capability-manifest.spec.ts:75` accepts `UPDATE_LINE_AI_CAPABILITY_MANIFEST=1`; that is an existing artifact-maintenance command, not a new runtime feature switch. This spec currently has **zero** literal registrations in `apps/api/package.json`; add no tests there and do not edit package.json in this round.

Impact by channel:

| Reader/writer | This change | Invariant / failure risk |
|---|---|---|
| Web | Fixed labels, accessible names, notices and existing display helpers | Selectors, guide quotations and button width can break; no hidden/opened grant |
| API | Existing customer-message helpers only, stable codes/payloads | A shared word may also feed export/snapshot; freeze those before edits |
| LINE | Existing action/read/fallback formatters and Thai→English fixed lookup | Signed card, enum token, classifier input and final mutation are unchanged; don't claim all actions are unavailable |
| AI Gateway | Fixed own-record explain/persona/help presentation | No prompt edits/provider rerouting/model-call changes; failed/unusable AI call remains uncharged by the separate owner step 2 fix |
| Exports/tax/print | Read-only regression evidence | No new wording, rates, formulae, master labels, filenames, filing status, paper layout or regenerated stored PDF |
| Notifications | Existing templates and stored notices, actor/assignee/data owner | Never add the shop owner as an automatic recipient; bank-account notices remain about the data owner |
| Journeys | Update UI selectors in the same round | Export assertions at `expense-input-vat-right.journey.spec.ts:261` must **retain** accounting headings |

Whole-chain acceptance: send an ordinary receipt and a full VAT invoice through LINE; inspect the draft, review, locked/failure and signed-action states on web; record payment; review voucher/50 ทวิ eligibility; view the concerned person's notice; export the existing accountant files; confirm plain words on web/LINE and exact words/identical monetary values in downloaded files. Repeat with lower pack versus 1,999, reviewer versus sender, and no permission. No real filing/payment/provider spend is required for wording proof.

## 9. Owner answers (2026-11-04)

| Question | Answer / bounded effect |
|---|---|
| Q1 — **answered 2026-11-04** | Show “หลักฐานว่าบันทึกจ่ายแล้ว” once on the paid voucher view; keep the title “ใบสำคัญจ่าย”; P3.1 no longer waits for an answer. |
| Q2 — **answered 2026-11-04** | Approved as recommended: before release the controller shows the owner the real rendered C53–C56/C62/C71 help cards, and the owner approves those rendered cards then; P5.3/P5.4/P6.2 retain this release gate. |
| Q3 — **owner instruction 2026-11-04** | “ภงด.53 ต้องยื่นเมื่อไหร่” in LINE answers the date directly instead of pointing to the web page. Rules: ① use the same due date the withholding page shows for that month (`pnd53PaperDueDay`: configured period date, else the 7th of the next month); ② no month named → the paper whose due date is today or later (today on/before last month's due date → last month, otherwise this month), Bangkok civil date; a named month → that month; ③ needs `wht.pnd53.read` and a paid shop — others keep today's refusal with its way out; ④ no model call is added; ⑤ the `pnd53` month summary always states its due date too, including for readers who can see certificates. Verified in `erp-tax-tools.use-case.spec.ts` (PWS-PND53-DUE) and the registered webhook spec. |
| Q4 — **owner instruction 2026-11-04** (“เรื่องไหนที่ตอบได้ก็ควรตอบได้เลย”) | Controller re-check found two classes. **(a) Answer replaced by an unrelated offer:** `semantic-turn-v2.ts` `helpFor` swaps the selected card's answer for the linked `LINE_CHAT_ACTIONS` sentence, so “ใบเบิกคืออะไร”, “ทำไมเห็นแท็บเบิกจ่ายไม่เหมือนกัน”, “ทำไมฉันเห็นรายงานน้อยกว่าเจ้าของ”, “รายจ่ายเข้าระบบแล้วทำอะไรต่อ”, “ดูรายการตามสถานะที่ไหน” get a create/search/report offer instead of the answer (only 50 ทวิ/ใบสำคัญจ่าย/ภ.ง.ด. “คืออะไร” escape via `documentExplanationTopic`). Rule: a help question gets the card's answer, with the linked action kept as its button; the action sentence alone answers only a request to do the action (“ช่วย…ให้หน่อย”, the action command, or a router `howToActionId` without a selected card). **(b) “Go look” where the shop's data is readable:** answer with the reader's own data through the existing permission-checked tools, one fixed sentence, no added model call, card answer kept as fallback when the data cannot be read: `package.quota` → receipts left this round and when it resets (`billing.get_package_usage`); `package.renew`/`package.expiry` → current package end date (same tool); `claim.approve` → claims waiting for this reader (`reimbursement.list_pending_approvals`); `voucher.approve` → vouchers waiting for this reader (`voucher.list_recent`, only if it can filter to the reader's queue — otherwise keep the card); `notifications.read` → open tasks for this reader (`notifications.list_open_alerts`); `pnd53.prepare` → this month's ภ.ง.ด.53 state and due date (`wht.get_month_summary`); `wht.pnd53-due` per Q3. A reader without the data permission or feature gets the card's general answer (a help question is not refused). |

The 2026-10-26 instruction authorizes designing ordinary fixed LINE/bell wording despite the 2026-10-13 defer decision (`sold-ux-owner-decisions-2026-10-13-plan.md:22`, `:110`); it does not undo approved document titles, recipient rules or help review. LINE keeps “แดชบอร์ด”, the web keeps “หน้าแรก” (`:120`). Do not reopen settled claims signing, one-press expense payment, owner self-approval or employee-role names.

## 10. Classified source-line ledger — exhaustive for sold surfaces as of 2026-11-04 (continued in §16/§17)

Every original candidate is resolved as **S** (visible on a sold surface), **O** (out of scope: only unsold feature/hidden accounting/output), or **N** (not customer output). The catalog represents unique source file/line keys. S rows are expanded. O/N rows are grouped below by file and class, with every original I alias mapped to its source line. A mixed source line is S when it contains a displayed title/label; matcher keys or question aliases on that line remain unchanged. K means retain the complete current text; it does not authorize an edit. D denotes an original open wording item whose full resolution is in §11.

## 11. Resolution of all 97 formerly open D occurrences

All 97 original aliases are resolved below (95 unique source lines). Closed S supplies complete new customer text; closed O/N retains the existing literal because it is not a sold output. No open D remains. Labels are kept short rather than padded into sentences. Dynamic placeholders use the existing validated values.

For error/lock rows, retain the actual field/edit/back control. If the actor cannot fix it, show a quiet “ดูวิธีแก้” link to the existing expense guide `#detail` or claim guide `#stuck`; the guide names the responsible person and existing support command. LINE retains an authorized record button, otherwise the same-card contact command in B6. A toast alone does not pass refusal acceptance. No new permission or mutation is introduced.

Tax reason constants exported by `apps/api/src/modules/expenses/domain/logic/expense-export.logic.ts:235` keep their exact bytes: use the existing web reason-label seam `apps/web/src/features/expenses/expenses.model.ts:1047`. Unsold producers are untouched. A shared lock code must not promise that a filed paper can be unlocked.

## 12. Notification writers and role-template enumeration

R8/R10 apply to emitted text, not merely the bell's presentation. S means sold recipient-visible, O means unsold/hidden, N means internal. Source lines below are distinct occurrences, not duplicates added to the I ledger. Retained paper names follow W15–W25. No recipient, severity, trigger, cooldown, dedup key, actor exclusion or deep link changes in a wording round. Arbitrary names/reasons remain user data and are not rewritten.

## 13. B6 and B7 — separate minimal corrections, fully specified

### 13.1 B6: empty WHT catalog must not point to the staff console

Failing input: a sold expense needs whtCategoryCode and the active catalog is empty. `line-expense-action.service.ts:610` returns `/system/wht-income-categories`; the webhook URI branch `process-line-webhook.use-case.ts:12656` renders it. The calculator independently returns the same staff URL in `erp-advisor-document-tools.use-case.ts:141`. A shop cannot repair the platform catalog.

Smallest fix, P5.B6 (R2/R10, A10): in the action service's existing empty-catalog return remove webPath and replace reply with “ยังไม่มีรายการให้เลือก พิมพ์ ‘ติดต่อเจ้าหน้าที่’ ให้ทีมงานช่วยค่ะ”. In the calculator retain its existing readChoices shape, but set label “ติดต่อทีมงานมั่งมี”, text “ติดต่อเจ้าหน้าที่”, omit webPath; use the same one-sentence message. Existing `line-business-card.logic.ts:41`–`:45` supports a message choice; the support command already exists (`process-line-webhook.use-case.ts:2933` support pause, `:2964` request handling). Do not invent a permission, result DTO, support request, route or environment flag. No support request is created until the user chooses/sends the command. Remove the now-unused staff-button label branch only after the two producers are corrected; keep ordinary authorized expense links.

Tests first: registered `apps/api/src/modules/line-ai/application/use-cases/process-line-webhook.use-case.spec.ts` :: PWS-B6 empty catalog has an actionable support instruction and no system URI (new confirm, retained repair, no record mutation/no automatic support request); registered `apps/api/src/modules/line-ai/application/use-cases/erp-tax-tools.use-case.spec.ts` :: PWS-B6 calculator empty catalog returns support message choice. Verify outgoing Flex/quick replies, not only the tool result. API typecheck. This is a destination correction next to the wording round, with its own dependency/acceptance, not a grant to an unsold screen.

Impact/risk: API action/calculator produce; webhook and Gateway read existing choices; LINE delivery changes one destination. Web, tax calculations, stored categories, permissions, notifications, AI metering and accountant downloads do not change. Risk is a retained repair choice taking precedence over the support instruction; the empty-state test must include old continuation state and verify an actionable contact exit remains available. Design resolved; implementation unexecuted.

### 13.2 B7: reachable guide prose must follow the sold surface gate

Failing input: a 249 employee types `/companies/{id}/user-guide/tax` or `/user-guide/reports`. Tax page `apps/web/app/(workspace)/companies/[companyId]/user-guide/tax/page.tsx:13` always calls buildTaxGuide; tax guide `:21` promises VAT/WHT/CIT and `:35` displays ภ.พ.30. Reports page `.../user-guide/reports/page.tsx:12` always calls buildReportsGuide; its `reports-guide.content.ts:12` gates only the link. `web-core-workspace.logic.ts:214` allows unmatched guide paths. Therefore this prose is current sold-visible exposure, not an unsold-screen exclusion.

Smallest fix, P4.B7 (R1/R11, A1/A9/A11): use existing effective session permissions; in `buildTaxGuide` branch on existing full-tax read predicate at `tax-guide.content.ts:13`. Preserve the full builder for an actually entitled full-tax reader. For everyone else return a short sold WHT guide only: title “ภาษีหัก ณ ที่จ่าย”, description “ตรวจรายการที่หักภาษีไว้และเตรียมเอกสารให้ผู้รับเงิน”; include certificate/external-record instructions only with existing relevant wht grants, system issuance/signature only with existing systemCertificatesAvailable and company.tax_signature.manage, filing instructions only wht.pnd53.read. Omit VAT/CIT/rate-catalog/account-mapping/reconcile/report blocks and corresponding FAQs from this branch. No WHT read/manage grant: “เลือกคู่มือที่เปิดได้จากหน้าเริ่มต้น” with a link to existing getting-started guide. Keep lower-pack external 50 ทวิ recording; never promote it to system issuance.

In ReportsUserGuideRoute use the existing report.catalog.read/report.snapshot.read check before building the guide. Denied: reuse `common/components/state/ForbiddenState.tsx:24` with title “เปิดคู่มือนี้ไม่ได้”, description/helpText empty and action at `:49` linking to the existing getting-started guide (“กลับไปเลือกคู่มือ”). Do not pass packageAvailable=false, which would replace this title. Leave reports-guide.content.ts's formal report catalog and every unsold report/VAT/ledger screen unchanged. These are two guide seams, no new abstraction or permission. An existing entitled historical reader still gets original report content.

Tests first: `apps/web/src/features/user-guide/user-guide.content.spec.ts` :: PWS-B7 sold tax guide excludes unsold instructions (249/999/1999; requester/reviewer/finance; zero WHT grants; external/system distinction; historical full-tax reader unchanged); `apps/web/src/features/web-core-workspace/sold-features-regressions.spec.ts` :: PWS-B7 reports guide denies a sold typed URL with a working guide link. Extend existing journey `shop-menus-everyday.journey.spec.ts` and `withholding-forms.journey.spec.ts` with typed guide URLs at 390/1280/1556. Web typecheck. Guides are help-source-hashed: P5.3/Q2 must review the concrete changed source and P5.4 refresh reviewed metadata; do not fabricate approval or suppress hash failures.

Impact/risk: web guide route/builder only; existing session is the source, no data writer changes. LINE help's reviewed source fingerprint and W00 web-file hashes need their existing update path; all accounting output bytes, tax calculators, notifications, AI charging and module entitlements remain intact. Risk: filtering out lower-pack external recording, or treating an unsold route as sold through a raw permission; tests use effective sold-session grants and cover upgrade. Design resolved; implementation unexecuted.

## 14. Per-change impact and first proof

The following boundary key applies to each individual row, including the 95 unique D rows' Impact column in §11. The source data writer stays unchanged unless B6/B7 explicitly identifies a presentation destination/content branch. Tests named here are proposed additions to the existing full paths in §7/plan §3/§6. Each C row's concrete failing input and wrong output is in §4; no mass string replacement is authorized.

| Boundary | Reads/writes and whole-chain impact | What can break / first protection |
|---|---|---|
| F0 | O/N excluded; no changes in web, API, LINE, Gateway, output, tax, notice or journey | Accidentally exposing/rewriting unsold output; PWS-SOLD exclusion check |
| F1 | Web VAT display consumes API rights; shared contracts also feed export. Keep stored codes/conditions and exporter labels; LINE shared blocker selects existing plain mode only | Changing saved eligibility or output text; first PWS-VAT fixtures plus registered expense-export.logic.spec.ts PWS-OUTPUT |
| F2 | Web capture/selector/amount display only; API/Gateway facts, evidence, calculation, LINE actions, taxes and notices unchanged; update journey UI pins in same round | Confusing VAT/WHT base or treating zero as missing; first included/excluded/zero/custom-base fixtures and output comparison |
| F3 | Claims/shop/voucher web presentation; API writes bank/payment/status/audit as before; LINE names and guide agree, PDF and recipients unchanged | Stale bank destination, implied payment or lost permission exit; first frozen payout/lock/status cases, retained paper/title assertions |
| F4 | WHT web fields/common-error presentation; API tax codes and branches unchanged; LINE uses same concepts; filing/download/notifications unchanged | Global shared helper alters unsold UI or wrong filing advice; first filed/external/permission cases, no exporter edits |
| F5 | API fixed replies/action metadata → LINE cards/Gateway render; existing signed tools/mutations remain; no AI provider/prompt/charge, tax, export or recipient change | Raw enum leak, claiming action unavailable after it succeeded, wrong staff link; first outgoing-card branch tests; whole-chain P5.5 |
| F6 | Gateway fixed formatter/joke only; API tool facts, model prompts, charges, notifications and formal downloads unchanged | Formatting loses arithmetic or claims success; first grounded formatter fixtures and unusable-result existing billing assertions |
| F7 | Web guide/control quote + fixed help; whole-source hashes in approved help and W00 require review; no output or entitlement change | Publishing unapproved body/title/hash; first current approval/fingerprint parity; Q2 gates publication |
| F8 | API notification writer changes fixed words only; bell and LINE delivery read those same event strings; no web-only alias that disagrees with stored notice | Lost recipient/actor exclusion, trigger dedup or actual stage; first registered notice tests with exact audience and stage; no PDF edit |

The original catalog still represents 1,181 distinct source lines and 1,320 I aliases, with 139 duplicate literal fragments merged. Its accepted id set, current S/O/N classifications (including B27’s two unused-key corrections), all 97 D closures (95 distinct source lines), accepted B6/B7 designs and Q1/Q2 are preserved. Section 10 uses compact O/N rows with every old alias and source line. New occurrences use J ids. Requirements remain R1–R13 and acceptance A1–A13. Current change proposals are C01–C76.

Search terms are the complete list in §2, plus English tax/taxable, withholding, claimable, deduction/deductible, ledger, journal, debit, credit, payable/receivable, posting, fiscal, remittance, reconciliation, chart of accounts, income type and the new free/welcome/history control names. VAT and JV use whole-word token checks for the LINE source pass so CONSERVATIVE/OBSERVATION are not mistaken for tax text. English and Thai dictionary values are checked separately. Imported rendering seams inherit their owning label, and mixed source lines classify their displayed values separately from keys/regexes.

No controller audit folder remains unsearched. Customer-entered/custom names and source-document quotations are data at their existing rendering seams, as B9 records. Printed/exported accountant and tax terms, unsold module implementations and historical stored output words retain their defined scope boundary. The audit certifies fixed code text at the stated baseline. App acceptance still requires the focused tests, 390/1280/1556 browser proof, same-round journey updates and Q2's reviewed help version/hash publication.

The original round-2 519-file lexical pass is retained as historical search evidence. Round 3 adds the current route tree, renderer imports, both LINE modules, external notification writers and the current feature files named by the controller. Section 18 records exact re-anchor dispositions and verification, including the unavailable local tsx probe. All source reads and document edits were confined to this worktree, and edits remain confined to the two brief files.

## 16. Round 3 — dictionary, public help and LINE source audit

Audit date 2026-11-04, develop e7c422802. S includes the eligible never-paid free-draft audience F and paid packs P. P1999 means the current 1,999-only system withholding feature. Conditions and exact audiences below determine whether a row can appear. Existing I, D, C, R, A and B ids are retained. New occurrence ids J are additive. Counts for O/N represent source lines or dictionary entries as specified, with an opened example for each file. A mixed line is S when it also contains emitted text. Stored enum values, regex inputs, document transcription and customer names retain their source meaning.

### 16.1 Dictionary entries

Read the complete current `workspace-dictionary.ts`, including both locales and shared word objects. Classification is by its rendering consumer, with `workspace-i18n.logic.ts:4` selecting the requested locale and `:5` falling back to Thai. The inventory applies §2's accounting/tax terms to values, including English tax equivalents. Ordinary labels with no searched term are outside the jargon candidate set. An English customer label is S when the same control is reachable in the English locale. A source literal is inventoried once even when several controls consume it.

Opened reachability seams include `apps/web/src/features/auth/LoginPage.tsx:95` for auth.hero.sub, `apps/web/src/features/expenses/ExpensesPages.tsx:3347` for manual tax words, `apps/web/src/features/web-core-workspace/web-core-workspace.constants.ts:243` for module.pnd53, and `apps/web/src/features/crm/Customer360Page.tsx:38` for unsold customer paper labels. The CRM erasure text belongs to that same unsold page at `:559`/`:592`. The paid route list in §17 and the current free route predicate determine the audience. Already accepted I rows retain their classification. New S entries are fully listed here.

Dictionary audit read 3843 literal entries. The search matched 228 entries when the new free-mode keys are included. 55 additional S entries are expanded above. Additional O entries in this file total 9, for unsold module/customer consumers. One opened example is `apps/web/src/common/i18n/workspace-dictionary.ts:549` — “สมุดรายวัน”. The source keys and import names are N. This count excludes ordinary values with no searched term. Existing accepted dictionary I rows remain in §10.

The smallest edits are the existing dictionary values consumed by their current controls. C68 belongs to the buyer-mismatch cases in P1.3. C69 belongs to capture/manual/category/substitute help in P2.2–P2.4, with the category and substitute views checked in their existing round. The bilingual pairs share the existing t() lookup. F never acquires a reviewer, confirmation or payment control through a label change. The read-only buyer branch receives reviewer guidance. Tax bases, eligibility answers and printed/export labels are checked by A3/A4/A12 before changing their words.

### 16.2 Public wiki and reviewed question summaries

`apps/web/app/llms.txt/route.ts:2` returns LLMS_SUMMARY and `apps/web/app/llms-full.txt/route.ts:2` returns LLMS_FULL. These publicly readable answers are S when they describe sold work, even though they are plain-text routes. The imported question snapshot is also a text producer, through `home-wiki.ts:88`. Reviewed titles/hash are pinned in `home-help-v6.spec.ts:18`–`:26`. Q2 covers the reviewed snapshot revision as well as the LINE help revision.

Public O counts are kept compact. `home-wiki.ts` has one standalone O report-exclusion line at `:24` and O report clauses in `:55`/`:76`, each retained. `home-help-v6.json` has two O report cards (eight title/answer fields), retained. The opened example is the report.access answer, found by its id in that file, which says reports are outside the sold packs. `home-help-v6.spec.ts:77` explicitly pins that exclusion. The public wiki contains no affirmative promise that sold packs include reports, ledger, CRM, inventory or bank reconciliation. C70's new issue is the obsolete blanket “business work starts after payment” sentence at `home-wiki.ts:51`/`:72`, which omits the merged free source-draft path. B20 records it below.

C70 edits LLMS_FULL's own paragraphs, and C71 updates only the reviewed web snapshot through Q2 with the existing active help version/hash review. C70/C71 readers are the public wiki routes, FAQ snapshot pins and LINE help parity checks. The accountant exports and tax papers consume their existing contracts separately. Risks are an invented free-chat capability, a stale paid-only promise, or a reviewed snapshot diverging from its active help titles. P6.1/P6.2 test both free eligibility and paid package availability using the current catalog, with existing public-home journey coverage in §18.

### 16.3 Model, OCR, prompt, regex and fixed LINE text

Read every production TypeScript file under both LINE modules (152 files), with §2's terms and whole-word English VAT/JV matching to avoid CONSERVATIVE/OBSERVATION token false positives. The pass found 102 additional source-line hits beyond the accepted ledger and current cited seams. Emitted strings are expanded below. For each remaining file the table counts O/N additional hits and gives an opened example. Prompts and OCR transcription describe facts or machine outputs. They are N. C72 is the narrow prompt exception because its instruction explicitly tells the model which words to say to a customer.

### 16.4 Current free path, welcome/advisor and activity history

The free audience derives from `apps/api/src/modules/system-partner-management/application/free-expense-access.ts:12` and its historical purchase checks at `:28`–`:49`. `freeExpenseDraftPermissions` at `:67` intersects existing grants. `isFreeExpenseDraftRoute` at `:72` permits source draft reads/edits, including history, while `expense-commercial-access.ts:22` refuses financial actions. The shared monthly admission uses `free-expense-quota.ts:21` and counts persisted usable results at `:48`–`:53`. These rules come from the merged free spec D02/D04/D06, not from the old trial label FREE_TRIAL_15. Existing trials and already-paid expired shops retain their separate paths.

At `process-line-webhook.use-case.ts:6933` the free branch returns deterministic text before the model route. The model-free command is exactly “เมนูหลัก” (`line-staff-menu.logic.ts:881`). F receives upload/list/source draft/history, account-boundary, support and package entry text. Reviewer decisions, payment, claims, voucher issuance and system withholding remain P cases. Every R requirement's path is stated in §18. Wording changes must test F and P, including F→P upgrade and a quota-exhausted editable draft.

History N counts are six action-label entries filtered by the API before pagination, plus two system/service actor-label alternatives. Opened examples are `apps/api/src/modules/expenses/infrastructure/prisma-expense.repository.ts:108`–`:110` (automatic action exclusions) and the existing actor helper in `expense-history.logic.ts`. Those stored action names and API projection rules remain exact. A human expense.void still renders its label and is C73. History fields/before-after data are not rendered under the owner's concise-history decision. C73 therefore edits the two visible action-label values in expenseHistoryActionLabel, with current permission, pagination, actor and document-number checks.

Free quota DTO/error codes are N. The customer copy above reads their existing successful count, reservation count and resetAt. WelcomeMessage branches on actual zero-used/no-in-flight, remaining>0, in-flight, or exhausted allowance. It sends one creation-fenced welcome through the existing outbox slot, after active membership and LINE binding checks (`line-signup-notify.service.ts:402`–`:428`). The welcome's owner is the newly created shop's initiating data owner. The advisor uses the current public billing catalog, four selected answers and a refreshed offer before handoff (`line-package-advisor.page.ts:140`, `:349`). Its result deliberately omits VAT under the newer owner decision in `docs/specs/new-shop-welcome-package-advisor-spec-th.md:55`. C70/C71 public words must respect that product boundary as well.

C74 changes the free question refusal and free upload hints at their existing literal owners. The refusal gives the existing model-free “เมนูหลัก” command as an immediate way forward. The upload hints describe preparing a draft. Permission, access, file-state and quota refusals keep their own reason. First prove zero model/planner call, zero final mutation and no paid-credit consumption for F, then check the paid signed-action branch still produces its real result. The successful third read and blocked fourth read remain distinct, and an unusable result releases admission through the current quota pipeline. This wording work changes no counter or settlement rule.

## 17. Route tree and remaining API notification writers

### 17.1 Every reachable customer page route at the current baseline

Read all 240 `.ts`/`.tsx` route-tree files, including layouts, handlers and platform exclusions. The following table lists each reachable customer page once, including redirect aliases, public/legal/account boundaries and current unsold-content exposures. Route-local words and imported renderers were searched with §2's terms. A file already in §10/§16 inherits its literal inventory. A listed entrypoint with no matching fixed term is covered by the zero-hit lexical review and its shared dictionary/child renderer. The additional reachable matching words are expanded below.

Paid gates come from route-permissions.ts and effective session permissions, then `WorkspaceLayout.tsx:326`/`:366`. `feature-gate.logic.ts:126` strips unsold permissions, while `:132` retains narrowly scoped report-history grants. `COMPANY_PERMISSION_KEYS` at permission-registry.ts:487 includes those retained reads for an owner. Free gates run first at `web-core-workspace.logic.ts:177` and use `:81`'s path predicate, with the separate billing-manage branch. The route table therefore distinguishes F from P instead of reusing the old paid-only screen list.

There are 74 customer page routes in the table and 129 excluded page routes. Excluded pages stay grouped by their existing platform role, unsold permission or development-only fixture. One opened example is `apps/web/app/system/layout.tsx:25` (platform access check), and another is `apps/web/app/(workspace)/companies/[companyId]/billing/preview/page.tsx:7` (local/development predicate). Unsold settings use the exact route-permission rule before their renderer. /ai-suggestions additionally needs an unsold source grant from `packages/contracts/src/ai-draft-suggestion.ts:366`–`:375`, so the ai_line_assistant entitlement alone does not make that screen sold-reachable.

The public text routes `/llms.txt` and `/llms-full.txt` are S and fully classified in §16.2. `/l/[token]` is an authenticated redirect handler. `/api/auth/*` and `/api/public/billing/catalog` are transport handlers whose error presentation is already the common API/auth surface. `robots.ts`, `sitemap.ts`, CSS, metadata and loading/error/not-found wrappers were also opened. Fixed visible wrapper text falls under common auth/state and the same term scan. API-served LIFF advisor routes are covered by §16.4, including `/v1/auth/line/link-page/package-advisor` and `/v1/auth/line/package-advisor-page`.

## 18. Current-path acceptance and implementation delta

### 18.1 Requirement audience matrix

F means an eligible never-paid free source-draft shop. P means a shop on an active paid 249–1,999 pack. A paid-only workflow requirement also proves the free guard and safe refusal, so a word change cannot expose that workflow to F. Old free trials and expired paid shops retain their own existing state cases.

| Requirement | F path | P path | Current evidence / plan / test / acceptance |
|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | Existing never-paid eligibility, 3 usable source reads per month, restricted route and grant intersection | Starter ceiling, extra system WHT on 1,999 | `free-expense-access.ts:12`, `:67`, `:72`; P0/P2.F; registered webhook spec :: PWS-FREE-WORDS; A1 |
| R2 | Plain upload/quota/refusal/menu/history words and immediate model-free exit | Shared labels, refusals and paper-name explanations | `free-expense-line.logic.ts:1`; P1–P6; one-source-words.spec.ts :: PWS-DICTIONARY-AUDIT; A2 |
| R3 | Source draft facts only, review/decision actions remain guarded | Reviewer VAT decision and read-only/immutable cases | `ExpenseReviewPage.tsx:1037`, `:1065`; P1.2/P1.3; rendered review spec :: PWS-VAT; A3 |
| R4 | Stored source amounts and draft edits use current validation, zero/unknown remain distinct | Existing VAT/WHT base, rates and overrides | `expenses.model.ts:1627`; P2.1/P2.2; rendered review spec :: PWS-MONEY; A4 |
| R5 | Receipt upload/list/editable source draft, allowance and history | Full paid capture/manual/list/category/evidence variants | `ExpensesPages.tsx:3404`, `ExpenseHistoryModal.tsx:13`; P2.F/P1.3-H; ExpensesPages.qa.spec.ts :: PWS-FREE-COPY; A5 |
| R6 | Safe refusal, existing source draft remains usable | Prepare/approve/pay voucher states and retained voucher title | `expense-commercial-access.ts:22`; P3.1 and registered webhook spec :: PWS-FREE-FINANCIAL-DENIAL; A6 |
| R7 | Claim and payout mutations remain guarded, no route through copy | Bank account save, masked data and frozen paid claim destination | `free-expense-access.ts:58`; P3.2; reimbursement-saved-payout.spec.ts :: PWS-PAYOUT; A7 |
| R8 | Own account-boundary/support/package paths and new initiating owner's creation-fenced welcome | Current notification assignee/data-owner templates and employee/settings views | `line-signup-notify.service.ts:402`, `:427`; P3.3/P6.3; registered signup-notify spec :: PWS-WELCOME-SOURCE; A8 |
| R9 | No financial certificate or filing action admitted | Lower-pack external record and 1,999 system issuance/PND3/53 | `free-expense-access.ts:74`; P4; WithholdingPage.spec.ts :: PWS-WHT; A9 |
| R10 | Typed questions are refused before a model call, existing deterministic menu/support continues | Fixed cards, persona, signed preview result and replay handling | `process-line-webhook.use-case.ts:6933`; P5.1/P5.2; registered webhook spec :: PWS-FREE-WORDS and PWS-LINE-SOURCE-AUDIT; A10 |
| R11 | Public wiki/advisor and model-free help reflect actual eligibility | Existing guides, active help titles/bodies and source review | `home-wiki.ts:51`, `line-package-advisor.page.ts:140`; P5.3/P6.1–P6.3; home-help-v6.spec.ts :: PWS-WIKI-FREE-PAID; A11 |
| R12 | Source draft state does not acquire a download/financial capability | Formal accountant and paper bytes remain the output baseline | `free-expense-access.ts:75`; P1.1/P5.5; registered expense-export.logic.spec.ts :: PWS-OUTPUT; A12 |
| R13 | F upload→draft edit→history→quota/refusal→upgrade, model-free throughout the free leg | Existing LINE→review→pay→notice→accountant file chain | `ExpenseReviewPage.tsx:2019`, `process-line-webhook.use-case.ts:3729`; P5.5/P6.5; existing journeys plus registered webhook proof; A13 |

### 18.3 Document verification and acceptance boundary

Re-opened all cited source files and remapped the old line coordinates with exact old/current source matching. The original I ledger has zero excerpt-to-current-line mismatches after I0317's current-text disposition. Every original I alias, all 97 D closures, C01–C67, R1–R13, A1–A13, B1–B19 and Q1/Q2 remain represented. O/N source rows are compacted by file/class with every original id@line mapping. New C68–C76 cover the additional wording changes.

Read-only source checks validate path existence, line bounds, short evidence excerpts, table column counts, id preservation, duplicate source-line handling and API test registration. A read-only Node helper probe could not start because this checkout has no installed tsx package (`ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND`). No dependency installation was attempted. App specs, typechecks and browser checks remain planned implementation proof. This documents round creates neither application code nor a database migration. Existing schema/Prisma work is outside the wording change and any later authorized migration must be additive.
